### [35] [Harnessing Vision-Language Models for Perceptual Quality Assessment and Autonomous Content Adjustment in Augmented Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00677)
*Elias Rotondo,Lin Duan,Yanming Xiu,Sangjun Eom,Conrad Li,Maria Gorlatova*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的AR内容自动评估与优化框架，构建RateAR基准并验证VLM预测与人类主观评分高度一致，进而实现自动化AR内容调整系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AR头显在场景几何受限、空间抖动和时间不稳定等方面影响用户体验，而传统用户研究成本高、扩展性差、灵活性不足，难以支撑迭代式应用设计，因此需要自动化的AR视觉质量评估方法。

Method: 首先构建RateAR基准，收集多样场景与环境条件下的AR图像和视频，涵盖物体放置、尺度、阴影一致性等感知因素，信度达ICC(2,5)>=.90；随后评估11个商业VLM在该基准上的表现，并进行提示策略消融研究；最后基于发现构建自动化AR内容调整系统并开展21人用户研究。

Result: VLM质量预测与人类主观判断强相关，Spearman秩相关最高达0.8695；上下文提示相比其他提示策略与人类评分对齐更好，并平衡了引入的复杂性线索；21名参与者中超过90%认为系统改善了虚拟内容的放置与尺寸一致性。

Conclusion: 基于VLM的自动化框架可有效预测AR视觉保真度并支持内容优化，为替代或补充传统用户研究提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Advancements in augmented reality (AR) continue to foster innovative solutions, facilitating novel methodologies within educational systems, healthcare delivery, and risk-mitigation protocols. However, optimizing for end-user immersion and comfort remains challenging, as AR head-mounted displays contend with constrained scene geometry, spatial jitter, and temporal instability. User studies are the standard AR evaluation method for visual quality, but their cost, diminishing scalability, and inflexibility pose bottlenecks during iterative application design. To address this problem, we present an automated framework for AR content evaluation and refinement, built on vision-language models (VLMs), to evaluate and predict the visual fidelity of AR scenes as perceived by users. First, we introduce RateAR, a benchmark of AR images and videos collected across diverse scenes and environmental conditions, with good-to-excellent reliability (ICC(2,5) >= .90) across perceptual factors, including object placement, scale, and shadow consistency. Subsequently, we evaluate eleven commercial VLMs on the crafted benchmark. Results support that VLM-based quality predictions strongly correlate with human subjective judgments, achieving Spearman's rank-order correlations of up to 0.8695. An ablation study further suggests that, compared to other prompting strategies, our contextual prompting yields better alignment with human ratings while balancing introduced complexity cues. Building on these findings, we construct an automated AR content adjustment system and conduct a 21-participant user study. More than 90% of participants found that the system improved placement and size coherence of virtual content.

</details>


### [36] [SemanTok: Predictable Semantic Tokens for Efficient Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00686)
*Mikhail Dereviannykh,Vikram Voleti,Simon Donne,Mallikarjun Byrasandra Ramalinga Reddy,Shimon Vainer,Mark Boss*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SemanTok，一种灵活长度视频分词器，通过在编码器输入冻结DINO特征并为每个token前缀添加轻量重建头，实现各AR规模下的高语义对齐与高视频保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵活长度coarse-to-fine分词器仅在早期解码器隐状态上施加表示对齐(REPA)损失，而解码器可部分从加噪输入满足该目标，导致语义对齐（尤其在纯噪声等噪声水平下）不足。作者希望提升分词器在所有token前缀和噪声水平上的语义对齐与保真度。

Method: SemanTok将冻结的DINO特征输入编码器，并添加轻量级头，使其能仅从每个保留的token前缀重建这些DINO特征，从而在粗到细的每个前缀上都强制语义对齐，避免解码器从加噪输入走捷径。

Result: 1) 201M的SemanTok AR模型匹配或超越3.4倍大的VideoFlexTok AR模型，更大SemanTok AR模型保真度进一步提升；2) 在分布外类别上保持语义对齐；3) 在每个噪声水平（含纯噪声）下解码器语义对齐更高；4) 重建与生成均表现良好；短token前缀预测更便宜且生成保真度更好，像素细节延后到后续token。

Conclusion: 通过在所有token前缀上直接对齐冻结DINO特征，SemanTok实现了高语义对齐与高视频保真度，并在各AR模型规模、分布外类别与各噪声水平下均表现优异，兼顾重建与生成效率。

Abstract: Recent video-based world models pair the scalability of autoregressive (AR) prediction with the visual quality of diffusion models. The choice of scene tokenizer is paramount for the optimal performance of each of these, both in terms of fidelity and semantics. Flexible-length, coarse-to-fine tokenizers yield exactly that: the first coarse tokens carry the clip's global semantics while later tokens further specify details. Existing flexible tokenizers only apply a representation-alignment (REPA) loss on early decoder hidden states, a target the decoder can partly meet from its noised input instead. We introduce SemanTok, a flexible video tokenizer that feeds frozen DINO features into its encoder and adds lightweight heads that reconstruct them from each retained token prefix alone. SemanTok achieves high semantic alignment and video fidelity at every AR model size: a 201M SemanTok AR model matches or beats a VideoFlexTok AR model $3.4\times$ its size, and larger SemanTok AR models further improve fidelity. It keeps semantic alignment on out-of-distribution classes and gives the decoder higher semantic alignment at every noise level, including pure noise. It performs well in both reconstruction and generation, and its short token prefixes are cheaper to predict and give better generation fidelity, with pixel detail deferred to later tokens.

</details>


### [37] [Soundwich: Video Generation with Layered and Controllable Audio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00691)
*Zhuo Ning,AmirHossein Naghi Razlighi,Sagi Polaczek,Daniel Cohen-Or,Ali Mahdavi-Amiri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Soundwich，一种免训练框架，将冻结的联合音视频流匹配模型转为生成与共享视频同步、可独立编辑的多个音轨（stem）的生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联合音视频生成模型通常只生成单一混音轨道，缺乏源级控制，与实际工作中语音、音乐、音效、环境声分轨编辑的流程不符。

Method: 免训练框架；生成多个带显式时间活动控制的独立音轨；引入共享场景表示在各音轨间传递全局音视频上下文以保持连贯与分离；将每个音轨与其对应视觉源进行跨模态交互路由以提升一致性。

Result: 实验与人类评估显示在时间控制、源分离和自然度上均有提升，各音轨与视频保持同步，并支持独立重定时、静音、替换或重混。

Conclusion: 在连贯的音视频生成中实现了灵活的源级编辑。

Abstract: Recent joint audio-video generative models can synthesize realistic videos with synchronized sound, but typically generate audio as a single mixed track. This limits source-level control and differs from practical audiovisual workflows, where speech, music, sound effects, and ambient sounds are represented as separate editable tracks. We introduce Soundwich, a training-free framework that transforms a frozen joint audio-video flow-matching model into a generator of multiple synchronized, independently editable audio stems coupled to a shared video. Soundwich generates separate audio stems with explicit control over their temporal activity. To keep separately generated sounds coherent, we introduce a shared scene representation that communicates global audiovisual context across stems while preserving their source-level separation. We further route cross-modal interactions between each audio stem and its corresponding visual source, improving audiovisual consistency. The resulting stems remain synchronized with the video and can be independently retimed, muted, replaced, or remixed. Experiments and human evaluations show improved temporal control, source separation, and naturalness, while enabling flexible source-level editing within coherent audiovisual generation. Code is available at https://github.com/CodyNing/Soundwich.

</details>


### [38] [FedMAD: Modulation-Aware Directional Aggregation for Federated Learning in Remote Sensing Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00693)
*Barış Büyüktaş,Begüm Demir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出面向遥感图像分类的个性化联邦学习框架FedMAD，通过分离全局共享表示参数与客户端特定适配参数，并结合调制感知的方向聚合策略，缓解异构数据分布下的性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在遥感领域受到关注，因其可在不直接访问本地数据的情况下实现跨去中心化遥感影像库的协作训练。但客户端间数据分布异构时（源于地理差异、季节变化、成像与大气条件不同），联邦学习性能显著下降，因此需要针对该问题的解决方案。

Method: 提出个性化联邦学习框架FedMAD：将全局共享表示参数与客户端特定适配参数分离，以在保持全局可迁移表示的同时保留客户端特定特征；通过在主干网络中集成轻量调制模块和本地批归一化层实现。全局共享参数在客户端间协作优化，客户端特定参数保留在本地。此外引入调制感知的方向聚合策略，根据本地调制更新的一致性动态调整各客户端的聚合重要性，从而抑制来自异构数据分布的冲突更新，增强适配行为一致客户端的贡献。

Result: 在BigEarthNet-S2和EuroSAT数据集上的实验结果表明，在异构遥感数据分布下，FedMAD相较当前最先进的联邦学习算法具有有效性。所提框架代码将在https://git.tu-berlin.de/rsim/fedmad公开。

Conclusion: FedMAD通过参数分离与方向感知聚合，有效应对遥感图像分类中联邦学习的异构数据分布挑战，并在标准遥感数据集上验证了其相对先进联邦学习算法的优势。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) has recently attracted increasing attention in remote sensing (RS) since it enables collaborative model training across decentralized RS image archives without requiring direct access to local data. However, FL performance significantly degrades when the data distributions between clients are heterogeneous, which often occurs due to geographical differences, seasonal changes, and varying image acquisition and atmospheric conditions. To address this challenge, in this letter, we propose a novel personalized FL framework (denoted as FedMAD) for RS image classification problems. The proposed framework separates globally shared representation parameters from client-specific adaptation parameters to preserve client-specific features while maintaining globally transferable representations. This is achieved by integrating lightweight modulation modules and local batch normalization layers into the backbone network. Although globally shared parameters are collaboratively optimized between clients, client-specific parameters remain local to preserve domain-specific feature characteristics. In addition, FedMAD introduces a modulation-aware directional aggregation strategy that dynamically adjusts the importance of aggregation for each client according to the alignment of local modulation updates. This allows the global optimization process to suppress conflicting client updates originating from heterogeneous data distributions while enhancing the contribution of clients with consistent adaptation behaviors. The experimental results obtained on the BigEarthNet-S2 and EuroSAT datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of FedMAD compared to state-of-the-art FL algorithms under heterogeneous RS data distributions. The code of the proposed framework will be publicly available at https://git.tu-berlin.de/rsim/fedmad.

</details>


### [39] [Personalized Image Generation with Reasoning and Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00737)
*Bo Ni,Ngoc N. Tran,Qinwen Ge,Franck Dernoncourt,Seunghyun Yoon,Samyadeep Basu,Sungchul Kim,Puneet Mathur,Nedim Lipka,Tong Yu,Yu Wang,Ryan A. Rossi,Tyler Derr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出首个基于用户历史记录的个性化图像生成统一基准（含两个任务与多维评测协议），并给出方法PEARL，在两项任务上平均提升15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个人化图像生成局限于基于精选视觉样例的条件合成，无法真正刻画"用户是谁"；而现实中用户个人语境更丰富，包含长期积累的评论、帖子、图像、标题与元数据，需要一个能利用这些历史来生成契合用户生活方式与审美偏好的图像生成器。

Method: 构建首个基于用户历史的个性化图像生成统一基准，包含两个互补任务：个性化场景生成（将给定物体置入反映用户偏好与生活方式的场景）和个性化创意生成（围绕指定主题生成符合用户审美与视觉身份的图像）；并设计多维评测协议，评估目标保真度、视觉质量、用户可区分性、与用户历史的语义对齐及任务特定效用。同时提出PEARL，将多模态推理器与冻结的图像生成器耦合，在交错的"推理—反思"循环中优化，并使用差分数据奖励。

Result: 在两项任务上，PEARL均优于强基线，在个性化指标上平均提升15%。

Conclusion: 该工作为基于用户历史的个性化图像生成建立了统一基准与评测体系，并验证了推理器与冻结生成器结合、配合差分数据奖励的PEARL方法在个性化生成上的有效性。

Abstract: Personalized image generation has remained narrowly focused on conditional synthesis from curated visual exemplars, rather than capturing who a user is. In practice, however, a user's personal context is much richer, comprising reviews, posts, images, captions, and metadata accumulated over time. A truly personalized generator should leverage this history to produce images aligned with the user's lifestyle and aesthetic preferences. To this end, we introduce the first unified benchmark for personalized image generation from user histories. The benchmark comprises two complementary tasks and a multi-axis evaluation protocol that assesses target fidelity, visual quality, user distinguishability, semantic alignment with the user's history, and task-specific utility. Grounded in real-world e-commerce and social media settings, the benchmark includes: (1) Personalized Scene Generation, which places a given object in a scene that reflects a user's preferences and lifestyle, motivated by personalized product presentation; and (2) Personalized Creative Generation, which generates a novel image on a specified topic that is faithful to a user's aesthetic and visual identity, motivated by social media content creation. We further propose PEARL, which couples a multimodal reasoner with a frozen image generator in an interleaved reason-reflect loop optimized with differential data reward. Across both tasks, PEARL outperforms strong baselines, achieving an average improvement of 15% across personalization metrics.

</details>

*Rezvan Joshaghani,Steven Cutchin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过在不同通道监督下训练3DGS并冻结几何参数重新估计外观，研究亮度和色度对3DGS学习表示的贡献，发现几何形成强烈由亮度主导但非完全依赖亮度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准3DGS从RGB监督中联合学习几何和外观，难以区分亮度和色度对学习表示的贡献，本文旨在通过解耦通道监督来理解二者的作用。

Result: 仅亮度学习的几何平均比RGB几何低0.085 dB；删除色度后可由强求解器在冻结几何上重新拟合至原质量甚至略优；高阶球谐对亮度贡献1.44 dB、对色度仅0.19 dB；仅色度监督的几何比仅亮度监督差3.9-5.5 dB，密度化解释了部分差距。

Conclusion: 标准3DGS的几何形成强烈由亮度主导但非亮度专属，大部分色度外观可在空间支撑形成后恢复。

Abstract: Standard 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) learns geometry and appearance jointly from RGB supervision, making it difficult to isolate how luminance and chroma contribute to the learned representation. We study this by training models under different channel supervision, freezing their non-appearance parameters (position, scale, rotation, and opacity), and re-estimating appearance with the same solver before comparing held-out reconstruction. Across eleven benchmark scenes with four independent runs each, geometry learned from luminance alone supports held-out reconstruction 0.085 dB below RGB-trained geometry on average. If chroma is deleted from a trained model, a sufficiently expressive solver can re-fit it on the frozen geometry to the original quality or slightly better. Higher-order spherical harmonics contribute much more reconstruction quality to luminance than to chroma, improving PSNR by 1.44 dB versus 0.19 dB on average, although on mirror-like surfaces hue does still change with viewpoint. The luminance advantage is even larger when geometry is being formed. Chroma-only supervision produces geometry 3.9-5.5 dB worse than luminance-only supervision after the same appearance solve; densification explains part of this gap. Overall, geometry formation in standard 3DGS is strongly luminance-dominated but not luminance-exclusive, and much of the chromatic appearance can be recovered after spatial support has formed.

</details>


### [41] [Video Evidence Indexing: Learning Where to Look from Video Previews for Token-Budgeted Long-Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00757)
*Haowen Guan,Shengzhi Li,Shichao Pei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 token-budgeted Video Evidence Indexing (VEI)，用低分辨率 Video Preview 全局覆盖，再构建紧凑高分辨率 Evidence Set 用于长视频问答，并配合特权自蒸馏训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频问答受限于视觉 token 成本高和当前 VLM 固定上下文宽度。长视频问题虽需广泛时间覆盖，但答案往往只由少数关键片段支撑，因此需高效定位证据片段并合理分配有限的高分辨率帧预算。

Method: 将 VEI 视为一个策略，联合解决证据定位（找问题相关时刻）和预算规划（决定高分辨率帧预算分配）。推理流程为：Video Preview 提供廉价全局覆盖，Video Evidence Indexing 构建 Evidence Set，Answer Generation 结合两者进行最终 VQA。针对缺少帧级监督，采用特权自蒸馏，由答案感知教师指导学生生成的索引轨迹；探索每帧 1/6/12/24 个视觉 token 的预览，并训练单一策略支持四种分辨率。

Result: 实验表明，在有限视觉预算下 VEI 提高了准确率，自蒸馏进一步提升了 QA 准确率和时间证据定位能力。

Conclusion: 通过低分辨率预览加高分辨率证据集的 token 预算化索引策略，并结合特权自蒸馏，可在有限视觉预算下提升长视频问答准确率与证据定位效果。

Abstract: Long-video question answering is limited by the high cost of visual tokens and by the fixed context width of current VLMs. A long-video question may require broad temporal coverage, but the answer is often supported by only a compact set of moments. To locate these moments efficiently, we propose token-budgeted Video Evidence Indexing (VEI): given a dense low-resolution Video Preview, the model constructs a compact high-resolution Evidence Set for final reasoning. We treat VEI as a policy that must jointly solve \textit{evidence localization}, which finds question-relevant moments, and \textit{budget planning}, which decides where to spend the limited high-resolution frame budget. We implement this idea with an inference pipeline: the Video Preview provides cheap global coverage, Video Evidence Indexing constructs the Evidence Set, and Answer Generation combines both inputs for final VQA. To address missing frame-level supervision, we adopt privileged self-distillation, where an answer-aware teacher guides the normal test-time policy on student-generated indexing traces. We explore previews at 1, 6, 12, and 24 visual tokens per frame, training a single policy that supports all four resolutions. Experiments show that Video Evidence Indexing improves accuracy under limited visual budgets, and self-distillation further improves both QA accuracy and temporal evidence localization.

</details>


### [42] [VTV-FM: Flow Matching through Variational Terminal-Velocity Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00785)
*Haoyang Jiang,Yuheng Li,Di Yang,Yanhai Xiong,Haipeng Chen,Yi He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VTV-FM，一种二阶流匹配框架，通过最小化加速度能量推导出静态数据缺失的终端速度，并给出闭式解，同时该变分构造还定义了OT配对代价与训练用的加速度目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配方法多采用一阶桥：一旦源样本与目标样本配对，路径即为恒定速度的直线运动，即使是OT改进配对，桥本身仍是线性的，难以建模曲线运动、加速度和方向变化。改用二阶相空间动力学是自然的补救方案，但学习该桥需要静态数据集无法提供的目标侧终端速度信息。

Method: 提出变分终端速度流匹配（VTV-FM）：这是一个二阶流匹配框架，通过最小化加速度能量来推导缺失的终端速度，从而为静态数据得到闭式闭合解；同一最小加速度变分构造还用于定义OT配对代价以及训练所用的加速度目标。

Result: 在低维数据集、PDE控制的物理场以及CIFAR-10上的实验表明，VTV-FM在传输几何和生成质量上均优于一阶及高阶流匹配基线。

Conclusion: VTV-FM通过最小加速度变分构造，解决了二阶流匹配中静态数据缺失终端速度的难题，并在多类任务上提升了传输几何与生成质量。

Abstract: Flow matching (FM) learns generative transport by fitting continuous-time motion from a simple source distribution to the data distribution. Most existing methods use first-order bridges: once a source and a target sample are paired, the path is a straight motion with constant velocity. FM with optimal transport (OT) improves the pairing, but the bridge itself remains linear, limiting its ability to model curved motion, acceleration, and changing directions. A natural remedy is to use second-order phase-space dynamics; however, learning the bridge requires target-side terminal-velocity information that static datasets do not provide. We propose Variational Terminal-Velocity Flow Matching (VTV-FM), a second-order FM framework that derives the missing velocity by minimizing acceleration energy, yielding a closed-form closure for static data. The same minimum-acceleration variational construction also defines the OT pairing cost and the acceleration targets used for training. Experiments on low-dimensional datasets, PDE-governed physical fields, and CIFAR-10 show that VTV-FM improves transport geometry and generation quality over first-order and high-order FM baselines.

</details>


### [43] [Paying for Too Many Tokens? Valid and Cost-Efficient Multimodal LLM Annotation with Simple Heuristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00809)
*Zhixi Zhu,Kristina Gligoric*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了短视频视觉语言模型标注中的成本节省启发式方法，在情感与主题分类任务上从准确率、下游推断有效性和token成本三方面分析，发现准确率与有效性会背离、模态价值不保证、成本可与视频长度解耦，并据此提出指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM可用于大规模视频标注，但成本极高：1fps处理60秒短视频需数百万token。研究者常用采样帧、图像网格压缩、单模态等启发式来降本，但尚不清楚哪些方法真正省成本，以及是否保留下游结论，故需系统评估。

Method: 在短视频上，针对两个计算社会科学任务（情感与主题分类），沿三条轴系统评估各配置：分类准确率、下游推断有效性、每视频token成本。这些轴在文献中通常被分开处理。

Result: 发现三点：1）准确率与有效性背离，最高准确率配置可能产生错误结论；2）模态价值不保证，仅文本即可获得强表现，增加模态可能增本不增信号；3）成本可与视频长度解耦，通过简单镜头切换检测构建单个2×8图像网格可接近全视频理解（κ差值在.05内），token成本约为15%。

Conclusion: 基于上述发现推导出指南，支持在计算社会科学中实现成本感知的VLM标注。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) enable video annotation at scale, but costs accumulate quickly: processing a typical 60-second short-form video at one frame per second requires millions of tokens. To reduce costs, researchers rely on heuristics such as sampling a subset of frames, compressing videos into image grids, or using only a single modality. However, it remains unclear which heuristics save cost, and whether they preserve the downstream conclusions these annotations enable. To address this gap, we conduct a systematic evaluation of these heuristics using short-form videos, on two computational social science (CSS) tasks: sentiment and topic classification. We evaluate each configuration along three axes the literature typically treats separately: classification accuracy, validity of downstream inference, and per-video token cost. First, we find that accuracy and validity diverge: the highest-accuracy configuration can produce wrong conclusions. Second, modality value is not guaranteed: text alone can yield strong performance, indicating that adding modalities can add cost without adding signal. Finally, we find that cost can be decoupled from video length when annotating short-form videos: a single $2\times8$ image grid built via simple shot-transition detection approaches full-video understanding ($κ$ within~.05), at $\sim 15\%$ of the token cost. Based on these findings, we derive guidelines that can enable cost-aware VLM annotation in CSS.

</details>

*Chaoyu Li,Xiaoyi Gu,Yogesh Kulkarni,Eun Woo Im,Mohammadmahdi Honarmand,Zeyu Wang,Juntong Song,Fei Du,Xilin Jiang,Kexin Zheng,Tianzhi Li,Fei Tao,Pooyan Fazli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是首篇关于视频生成模型后训练与对齐的系统综述，将后训练统一为框架，按对齐信号实施方式区分隐式与显式对齐，并把现有方法归为四类，同时梳理数据集、基准与评估实践及开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成已从短时低质片段发展到高分辨率、长时长且具复杂时空动态的序列。尽管大规模预训练赋予模型强生成先验，但预训练视频模型仍难以可靠遵循人类意图、保持时间连贯性并满足物理与安全约束。相较图像与文本生成，视频生成的对齐面临独特挑战：误差随时间累积、运动与外观耦合、多目标权衡、时间属性监督不足。这促使研究者采用系统化后训练策略，在不从头重训的前提下适配预训练模型，因此需要一篇系统综述加以梳理。

Method: 论文以综述形式展开。作者将后训练界定为统一框架，依据对齐信号如何被施加，区分隐式对齐与显式对齐。基于该视角，把现有方法组织为四大类：监督微调方法、自训练与蒸馏方法、基于偏好与奖励的方法、推理时方法。该分类体系刻画了对齐信号在训练与部署阶段如何塑造模型行为。此外，论文还综述了常用数据集、基准与评估实践。

Result: 论文构建了一个连贯的分类体系，统一呈现了对齐信号在训练与部署全过程中对模型行为的影响；并系统整理了对齐研究常用的数据集、基准与评估实践，为后续研究提供了结构化参考。

Conclusion: 论文讨论了可扩展奖励设计、长时程时间一致性、稳定性与表现力权衡、安全感知生成等开放挑战，旨在为推进可控且可靠的视频生成模型提供结构化概念基础与实践指导。

Abstract: Video generation has rapidly progressed from short, low-quality clips to high-resolution, long-duration sequences with complex spatiotemporal dynamics. Despite strong generative priors learned through large-scale pretraining, pretrained video models often fail to reliably follow human intent, maintain temporal coherence, or satisfy physical and safety constraints. Compared with image and text generation, alignment in video generation presents unique challenges, including error accumulation over time, motion-appearance coupling, multi-objective trade-offs, and limited supervision for temporal properties. These challenges motivate systematic post-training strategies that adapt pretrained models without retraining them from scratch. In this survey, we present the first comprehensive review of post-training and alignment in video generation models. We frame post-training as a unifying framework and distinguish between implicit alignment and explicit alignment based on how alignment signals are enforced. From this perspective, we organize existing approaches into four broad categories: supervised fine-tuning methods, self-training and distillation methods, preference- and reward-based methods, and inference-time methods. This taxonomy provides a coherent view of how alignment signals shape model behavior across both training and deployment. Beyond methodological advances, we review commonly used datasets, benchmarks, and evaluation practices, and discuss open challenges such as scalable reward design, long-horizon temporal consistency, stability-expressiveness trade-offs, and safety-aware generation. This survey aims to provide a structured conceptual foundation and practical guidance for advancing controllable and reliable video generation models.

</details>


### [45] [Align Then Reason: A Multimodal Lip-Sync Judge for Dubbing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00825)
*Rui Liu,Bhavin Jawade,Haoqi Li,Shivam Mehta,Karan Saxena,Yinghong Lan,Cameron R. Wolfe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Align Then Reason（ATR）无参考唇形同步评判器，先做帧级唇形表征与候选文本音素单元的单调对齐，再让 LLM 基于对齐证据推理，兼顾内容与时间；在七语言基准上大幅超越 SFT 基线，并泛化到其他 LLM 家族与未见语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配音质检需要在没有参考音频（配音音频尚未生成）的情况下，仅凭静音视频与文本判断候选台词是否与说话者可见口型在内容与时间上匹配。现有视觉语音识别器和视频-语言模型即便微调后能恢复口型内容，也对时间错误不敏感，难以胜任该任务。

Method: ATR 分两步：先用对齐打分器在帧级唇形表征与候选台词的音素单元之间建立单调对齐，为每个音素单元提供局部证据，同时给出校准过的全局对齐分数；随后 LLM（2B/4B/9B 等）基于这些对齐证据进行推理，联合判断内容与时间的一致性。

Result: 在七语言基准上，相比对应 Qwen3.5 SFT 基线，2B、4B、9B 推理器的平均 AUC 分别提升 59.4%、50.2%、50.8%；跨 LLM 家族泛化到 LLaMA-3.1-8B 与 Mistral-7B，分别较最优基线提升 45.9% 和 46.6%；并迁移到三个未见过的 MuAViC 语言。下游任务中，配音台词重排序上 ATR-9B 超最佳唇读基线 52.0%，脚本-片段匹配上提升 17.7%。

Conclusion: 通过"先对齐、后推理"的范式，把显式的时间对齐证据引入 LLM 判断，ATR 成为有效的多语言、无参考唇形同步评判器，在内容与时间双重敏感度上显著优于现有唇读与视频-语言基线，并具备跨模型、跨语言与跨数据集的泛化能力。

Abstract: Dubbing quality control requires a reference-free judge that can determine whether a candidate text line matches a speaker's visible articulation in both content and timing, using only silent video and text because dubbed audio may not yet exist. Existing visual speech recognizers and video-language models are poorly suited to this setting: even when fine-tuned to recover spoken content from lip motion, they remain largely insensitive to temporal errors. We introduce $\textit{Align Then Reason}$ (ATR), a multilingual lip-sync judge that first establishes a monotonic alignment between frame-level lip representations and the phonetic units of the candidate line, then reasons over this alignment to make the final judgment. An alignment scorer provides the LLM with both local evidence for each phonetic unit and a calibrated global alignment score, enabling it to reason jointly about content and timing. On a seven-language benchmark, our method improves mean AUC over the corresponding Qwen3.5 SFT baselines by 59.4%, 50.2%, and 50.8% with 2B, 4B, and 9B reasoners, respectively. The gains generalize across LLM families, reaching mean AUC improvements of 45.9% and 46.6% over the best baseline for LLaMA-3.1-8B and Mistral-7B, respectively. They also transfer across datasets to three unseen MuAViC languages. Furthermore, we evaluate on two downstream tasks built from real dubbing lines. On dub-line reranking, ATR-9B outperforms the best lip-reading baseline by 52.0%, while on script-to-clip assignment, ATR-9B improves over the best lip-reading baseline by 17.7%.

</details>


### [46] [Geometric Similarity in VLM Low-Level Vision Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00848)
*Shao-Jun Xia,Huixin Zhang,Zhen Lei,Anlan Sun,Yuner Zhang,Xiaoyang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了视觉语言模型（VLM）在低层次图像复原任务中的隐藏层表征组织规律，提出统一四层次分析框架GeoSim，覆盖24个低层次任务、5大类，用于分析AR模型与DiT的表征相似性及跨任务/跨模型迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM（含自回归模型与扩散Transformer）被视为通用视觉骨干的有力候选，但将其高效适配到"一体化"低层次图像复原仍具挑战。关键瓶颈在于：领域内尚不清楚VLM如何组织隐藏层表征，也不清楚结构迥异的AR与DiT范式是否共享用于像素级感知的共同几何组织。而这种共享组织是构建高可迁移、统一复原VLM与适配器的前提。

Method: 提出GeoSim——一个统一的四层次框架，从全局相似性、局部几何、稀疏特征分解、拓扑验证四个视角分析任务条件化表征。该框架适用于AR模型的隐藏状态与DiT的特征图，并支持同任务、跨任务、跨模型等多种设置。研究覆盖24个低层次任务、5个类别，进行系统性的表征相似性调查。

Result: 揭示了低层次视觉表征的组织原则，同时暴露了其在跨任务与跨模型一致性方面的局限。

Conclusion: GeoSim为探测低层次视觉中的潜在可迁移性提供了可解释性视角，并可用于诊断模型在任务特定或模型特定场景下的局限。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have emerged as powerful candidates for universal vision backbones, with representative architectures including autoregressive (AR) models and diffusion transformers (DiTs). Yet, adapting them efficiently for all-in-one low-level image restoration remains a challenge. Crucially, the field lacks an understanding of how VLMs organize hidden-layer representations and whether these structurally distinct paradigms share a common geometric organization for pixel-level perception. Such shared organization is a prerequisite for building highly transferable, unified restoration VLMs and adapters. In this paper, we systematically investigate representational similarity across 24 low-level tasks spanning 5 categories. We propose GeoSim, a unified four-level framework that analyzes task-conditioned representations from global similarity, local geometry, sparse feature decomposition, and topological verification perspectives. Our formulation applies to the analysis of hidden states in AR models and feature maps in DiTs across same- and cross-task/model settings. Our results reveal the organizing principles of low-level visual representations while exposing their limits in cross-task and cross-model agreement. Ultimately, GeoSim provides an interpretability lens for probing latent transferability in low-level vision and diagnosing model limitations in task- or model-specific scenarios.

</details>


### [47] [SmoothOperator: Enhancing Representations for Fine-grained Open-set Recognition via Modulated Label Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00851)
*Thiru Thillai Nadarasar Bahavan,Yu Xia,Sachith Seneviratne,Saman Halgamuge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SmoothOperator (SmoothOP)，一种即插即用的样本级标签平滑方法，根据样本的"突出度"(prominence) 动态设置平滑系数，集成到四种球形表示学习目标中，在语义偏移基准上取得最多 4.7% 的 AUROC/OSCR/闭集准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放集识别（OSR）的核心难点在于训练时无法建模无界的未知类分布。当前主流方法不直接建模未知类，而是塑造特征空间使已知类紧凑且分离，球形表示学习在此表现优异，其中标签平滑是关键成功因素之一。然而现有标签平滑对所有训练样本使用相同系数，忽略了每个样本的嵌入质量差异，这一"一刀切"的做法限制了性能。

Method: 作者首先证明 OSR 中的球形表示学习目标共享单一的 alignment–uniformity 结构，标签仅通过 alignment 项进入，因此标签平滑本质上是一个"对齐旋钮"(alignment dial)，固定系数意味着所有样本被设为同一数值。基于此，提出 SmoothOP：利用 embedding 空间的"突出度"(prominence)——衡量样本自身类别相对于最强竞争类别的显著程度——为每个样本单独设置平滑系数，突出度高的样本获得强平滑，从而降低其 alignment、放松其拉力。该方法可作为插件集成到四种现有球形表示学习目标中，训练开销极小。

Result: 在 Semantic Shift Benchmark 上，SmoothOP 增强的变体在多数情况下优于其基座目标，跨数据集、不同程度的语义偏移以及不同的 OSR 后处理器均表现一致，在 AUROC、OSCR 和闭集准确率上提升最高达 4.7%。

Conclusion: 将标签平滑重新解释为球形表示学习中的对齐调节机制后，固定系数显然并非最优；用样本自身的突出度来自适应调节平滑强度，可以在几乎不增加训练开销的前提下，稳定且显著地提升多种 OSR 方法的性能。SmoothOP 具有良好的通用性和即插即用特性。

Abstract: Open Set Recognition (OSR) aims to enable models to accurately classify known classes while rejecting samples from unseen classes. A key challenge in OSR lies in the inability to model the unbounded distribution of unknown classes during training, often leading to the misclassification of samples from these classes. Rather than modeling unknowns, recent work shapes the feature space so that known classes are compact and well separated, and spherical representation learning methods have achieved strong results this way. Label smoothing has been identified as one of the key drivers of this success, yet it applies the same coefficient to every training sample, regardless of how well each sample is already embedded. We show that the spherical representation learning objectives used in OSR share a single alignment--uniformity structure in which labels enter only through the alignment term. Label smoothing therefore acts as an alignment dial, and a fixed coefficient sets this dial to the same value for every sample. We propose a plug-in, SmoothOperator (SmoothOP), which sets the smoothing coefficient of each sample from its \textbf{prominence}, an embedding-space signal measuring how clearly the sample's own class stands out against its strongest competing class. Our method integrates into four existing spherical representation learning methods at minimal training overhead. SmoothOP assigns strong smoothing to samples with high prominence, which reduces their alignment and relaxes their pull. On the Semantic Shift Benchmark, SmoothOP-augmented variants generally outperform their base objectives across datasets, degrees of semantic shift, and OSR post-processors, with gains of up to 4.7\% in AUROC, OSCR, and closed-set accuracy.

</details>


### [48] [Lang3DSeg: Annotation-Free Open-Vocabulary 3D Segmentation with Point Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00855)
*Cigdem Kokenoz,Amir Salarpour,Alkim Domeke,Christopher Salas,Pedram MohajerAnsari,Long Cheng,Mert D. Pesé,Bing Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Lang3DSeg 提出以点变换器（point transformer）为骨干、无需人工标注的开放词汇室外 LiDAR 3D 语义分割方法，通过修正 2D 到 3D 投影的深度歧义问题，在 nuScenes 和 SemanticKITTI 上取得已发表无标注方法中最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室外 LiDAR 语义分割依赖封闭类别体系和昂贵的人工逐点标注。现有开放词汇方法虽避免标注成本，但几乎都基于体素稀疏卷积，点变换器仅用于数据密集且有界的室内场景，室外应用尚属空白。

Method: 以点变换器作为室外 LiDAR 开放词汇分割骨干，从零训练且不做几何预训练。针对 2D 到 3D 标签投影中的噪声（尤其是深度歧义导致物体后方的点被错误赋予该物体标签），采用显式类别优先级规则合成掩码，并在深度分布中遇到第一个间隙处截断投影实例，直接纠正投影误差而非在配准序列上取平均。

Result: 在 nuScenes 验证集上达到 52.8% mIoU，在 SemanticKITTI 上达到 41.4%，均为已发表无标注方法中的最高水平。每次 3D 语义分割基于单帧 LiDAR，推理实时进行而无需运行视觉语言模型。

Conclusion: Lang3DSeg 证明点变换器可作为室外开放词汇 LiDAR 分割的有效骨架，且通过显式投影纠错可在无标注、无几何预训练条件下超越现有方法，并保持实时推理效率。

Abstract: Accurate 3D semantic perception is critical for safe autonomous navigation. However, supervised LiDAR segmentation remains tied to closed taxonomies and to the cost of point-wise manual annotation. Open-vocabulary methods avoid that cost by projecting the output of 2D vision-language models onto LiDAR and distilling it into a 3D network. These methods rely almost exclusively on voxel-based sparse convolutions, and point transformers have so far been limited to indoor environments, where 3D data is dense and bounded. We present Lang3DSeg, which establishes a point transformer as the backbone for annotation-free open-vocabulary segmentation of outdoor 3D LiDAR, and is trained from scratch without geometric pre-training. This training paradigm necessitates addressing the inherent noise in 2D-to-3D label projections; specifically, naive projection often suffers from depth ambiguity, where points behind an object are erroneously assigned its semantic label. We therefore composite masks using an explicit class-priority rule and truncate each projected instance at the first gap in its depth distribution, correcting the projection error directly rather than averaging it over registered sequences. Lang3DSeg achieves 52.8% mIoU on nuScenes validation and 41.4% on SemanticKITTI, the highest among published annotation-free methods on both benchmarks. Every 3D semantic segmentation is on a single LiDAR sweep, and inference operates in real-time without running vision-language models.

</details>


### [49] [CtrlWAM: Controllable World Action Models with Aligned Intent and Foresight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00859)
*Chensheng Peng,Wenhao Ding,Ran Tian,Zewei Zhou,Jef Packer,Maximilian Igl,Peter Karkus,Yan Wang,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Boris Ivanovic,Marco Pavone,Yuxiao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CtrlWAM：通过在仿真器中执行扰动动作并配对其带噪视觉结果，解决世界动作模型中动作与视频联合去噪的不匹配问题，实现更可控的联合预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准世界动作模型（WAM）训练时对动作和视频同时加噪，但扰动动作对应的是反事实的未来视觉，而带噪视频仍绑定于真实录制，二者存在不一致；尤其低噪声下场景几何与动态行为仍清晰可见，导致动作与视觉预测脱节。

Method: 1) 在仿真器中执行扰动动作，并将其与对应的带噪视觉结果配对进行联合学习；2) 引入扭曲的视频–动作噪声调度，以适应视频与动作不同的去噪需求，保持视觉布局随动作预测演进而调整；3) 将动作接口从仅自我控制扩展为可变数量的智能体流，使统一模型能表示多个智能体的预测或指令未来。

Result: 驾驶实验显示动作预测更准确、生成视频与动作一致性更高、对给定指令的跟随更好；机器人实验显示运动保真度与可控性更强；匹配的对照实验支持离轨渲染对指令跟随与操作保真度的益处。

Conclusion: 通过仿真执行扰动动作并与视觉结果配对，结合扭曲噪声调度与多智能体动作接口，构建了更可控的世界动作模型。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) jointly predict actions (intent) and visual future (foresight). Standard training adds noise to recorded actions and video simultaneously, but such training paradigms introduce a mismatch: perturbed actions imply counterfactual future visual, while the noised video remains tied to the GT recording. In low-noise regime, the scene geometry and even the dynamic behavior remain clearly visible from the noisy future frames despite the added noise. We present CtrlWAM, which executes perturbed actions in a simulator and pairs them with their noised visual consequences for joint WAM learning. To accommodate the different denoising requirements of video and actions, we introduce warped video--action noise schedules that aim to keep visual layout responsive as action predictions evolve. We further extend the action interface from ego-only control to a variable number of agent streams, allowing a unified model to represent predicted or commanded futures for multiple agents. Driving experiments show more accurate action forecasts, closer agreement between generated video and actions, and better following of supplied commands; robotics experiments show stronger motion fidelity and controllability. Matched controls support the benefit of off-path renders for command following and manipulation fidelity. Together, these findings contribute to a more controllable world action model. Project page: https://ctrl-wam.github.io/

</details>


### [50] [Machine Translation for Sign Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00881)
*Ozge Mercanoglu Sincan,Anton Pelykh,Edward Fish,Harry Walsh,JianHe Low,Karahan Sahin,Oline Ranum,Sobhan Asasi,Steven Emery,Richard Bowden*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文综述手语机器翻译的发展，指出数据、评测与建模挑战，并强调技术难题需与利益相关者（尤其聋人社区）的伦理考量相平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手语机器翻译已从孤立手语识别发展到端到端翻译，但仍受限于数据集规模不足、评测指标无法充分反映语言质量，以及手语本身同时性、多层性、三维结构的建模困难，因此需要一篇兼顾技术挑战与利益相关者考量的综合综述。

Method: 采用跨学科综述方法，结合计算机视觉、手语语言学和聋人研究视角：分析手语在计算上的独特语言属性，梳理识别、翻译与生成系统的演进脉络，剖析尚存的技术挑战，并讨论数据治理、社区参与与合理使用等伦理议题。

Result: 综述系统整理了手语机器翻译的技术演进与现存瓶颈，并指出单纯技术推进不足以解决问题，需在数据、评测、建模与伦理治理等多个层面协同改进。

Conclusion: 手语机器翻译的持续进步依赖计算机视觉、手语语言学、聋人研究等领域的长期跨学科合作，以及与聋人社区的深度协作。

Abstract: Sign language machine translation has progressed substantially over the past decade, evolving from isolated sign recognition to end-to-end translation systems. Advances in pose estimation, transformer architectures, and large-scale dataset collection have driven progress, yet challenges remain. Datasets are limited compared to spoken-language resources; evaluation metrics inadequately capture the linguistic quality of output; and models must capture the simultaneous, multi-layered, and three-dimensional structure of sign languages. This manuscript provides a comprehensive review that seeks to balance technical challenges with stakeholder considerations. We examine the linguistic properties that make sign languages computationally unique, trace the evolution of recognition, translation, and production systems, and analyze ongoing technical challenges. Crucially, we address ethical considerations around data governance, community involvement, and appropriate use. Drawing on interdisciplinary perspectives spanning computer vision, sign language linguistics, and deaf studies, our analysis emphasizes that continued progress requires sustained collaboration across these fields and with deaf communities.

</details>


### [51] [EyeTAG: Eye Trajectory-Aware Gaze Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00922)
*Jungmin Lee,Niamat Ullah,Yoseob Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EyeTAG，一个显式建模注视轨迹的因果多帧视线估计框架，用一阶差分先验提供主体无关的运动信息，在 Gaze360 上平均角度误差降低约 1.0°，EVE 上与最强基线持平（2.56° vs 2.58°）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然头眼运动下的视线估计对驾驶员监控、人机交互等应用很重要。单帧方法逐帧独立预测导致输出抖动；多帧方法虽缓解抖动，但把运动隐式地学在表观特征里，注视轨迹从未成为显式变量。

Method: EyeTAG 是因果多帧框架，核心是显式的一阶注视先验：每一步对自身近期预测做差分，将得到的轨迹作为紧凑的运动学 token 反馈回去。由于差分的平移不变性，该 token 携带主体无关的运动而非个人注视偏移。人脸与眼部两路流提供视觉证据，经交叉注意力融合，并由因果 Transformer 解码器处理。

Result: 在 Gaze360 上平均角度误差降低约 1.0°；在 EVE 上达到 2.56°，与最强基线的 2.58° 相当。模型内消融（固定编码器与其余结构、仅改变注视历史）表明：去除该偏差的是差分形式本身，而非单纯的时序上下文——即使给定绝对注视历史先验，系统性扫视偏差仍存在。

Conclusion: 显式的差分轨迹先验优于隐式运动学习与绝对历史先验，能有效抑制抖动与系统性扫视偏差；代码已开源。

Abstract: Gaze estimation under natural head-eye motion underpins applications from driver monitoring to human-computer interaction. Single-frame methods predict each frame independently, so consecutive outputs fluctuate as jitter. Multi-frame methods reduce this, but they learn motion implicitly inside appearance features, so the gaze trajectory is never an explicit variable. We propose EyeTAG (Eye Trajectory-Aware Gaze Estimation), a causal multi-frame framework built around an explicit first-order gaze prior: at each step it differentiates its own recent predictions and feeds the resulting trajectory back as a compact kinematic token. Because differencing is translation-invariant in gaze space, this token carries subject-invariant motion rather than personal gaze offsets. Face and eye streams supply visual evidence, fused by cross-attention and a causal Transformer decoder. EyeTAG reduces the mean angular error by about 1.0$^\circ$ on Gaze360 and performs on par with the strongest baseline on EVE (2.56$^\circ$ vs. 2.58$^\circ$). Within-model ablations, which keep the encoder and the rest of the architecture fixed and vary only the gaze history, show that the differential formulation, rather than temporal context alone, removes the systematic saccade bias that persists even with an absolute gaze-history prior. Our code is available at https://github.com/peter8366/EyeTAG.

</details>


### [52] [Joint Branch-Space Transform Coding for Diffusion Activation Quantization with Classifier-Free Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00930)
*Mingrun Jiang,Yuejia Liu,Zishan Shao,Ting Jiang,Qinsi Wang,Hancheng Ye,Yixiao Wang,Rui-Feng Wang,Kangning Cui,Yixuan Chen,Fan Yang,Xiang Cheng,Hai Li,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出分支空间变换编码，利用CFG匹配激活的强相关性，推导出GCBT闭式解，在等比特预算下提升扩散模型后训练量化保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型后训练量化虽已利用时间步、特征与层结构，也引入CFG结构，但激活量化仍在条件与无条件坐标上独立进行，忽略了跨激活结构；作者发现匹配的CFG激活构成强相关的二维源，且分支编码基的选择会显著影响量化保真度。

Method: 提出分支空间变换编码，通过离线推导的2x2正交矩阵旋转匹配的CFG分支，仅需极小改动模型参数或量化流程；进一步推导GCBT，联合纳入CFG引导方向与跨分支二阶矩，在等速率量化噪声替代下得到逐层闭式解，无需梯度优化或角度搜索。

Result: 将GCBT叠加于现有扩散PTQ方法之上，在大多数对比中取得统计显著的保真度提升，且无统计显著退化，同时不改变原有宿主量化流程。

Conclusion: 匹配CFG激活的相关结构可被有效编码利用，GCBT以低成本闭式方案提升扩散模型PTQ性能，具备良好的通用性与兼容性。

Abstract: Post-training quantization for diffusion models increasingly exploits timestep, feature, and layer structure. While recent work has begun incorporating CFG structure into diffusion quantization, activation quantization still operates independently across conditional and unconditional coordinates, leaving cross-activation structure unexploited. We show that matched CFG activations form a strongly correlated two-dimensional source and that, under a fixed bit budget, the choice of branch coding basis materially affects quantization fidelity. Motivated by this observation, we introduce branch-space transform coding, which rotates matched CFG branches via an offline derived 2x2 orthogonal matrix, requiring minimal modifications to model parameters or the quantization pipeline. We further derive the Guidance-Correlation Branch Transform (GCBT), which jointly incorporates the CFG guidance direction and cross-branch second moments. Under an equal-rate quantization-noise surrogate, GCBT admits a closed-form per-layer solution without gradient optimization or angle search. Applied on top of existing diffusion PTQ methods, GCBT yields statistically significant fidelity gains in most evaluated comparisons with no statistically significant degradation, while leaving the underlying host quantization pipeline unchanged.

</details>


### [53] [A Matched-Budget Audit Framework for Recaptioned Image-Text Supervision Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00952)
*Giyeong Oh,Junghun Park,Yuhan Bae,Youngjae Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可复用的匹配预算审计框架，用五轴画像审计重标注图文语料的监督分布，并实例化验证发布语料优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重标注语料已成为T2I训练标准，但其监督分布受标注策略、标注模型和源语料共同影响；现有长度相关代理指标忽略标注语域伪影，下游T2I基准又与训练选择纠缠，难以在语料规模上审计该分布。

Method: 引入针对重标注监督分布 D_{π,V_c,C} 的可复用匹配预算审计框架，在固定文本预算 B=64 下输出涵盖提示侧覆盖度、图像条件忠实度和文本表面健康度的五轴画像，以可主张可控基本单元（CBU）为统一主张单位；在五个公开源语料上实例化七组配对比较。

Result: 在四个跨语料配对中，发布版本在 Qwen 与 Gemma 两种评判器下将每标题支持 CBU 提升 +3.39 至 +6.36；在 CC12M 上暴露出一致于两种评判器和四种预算的长-密前沿。

Conclusion: 该匹配预算审计框架可有效刻画与比较重标注监督分布；作者发布约 4.9 亿规模的已审计多源重标注语料及审计制品包。

Abstract: Recaptioned image-text corpora are now standard for text-to-image (T2I) training, with vision--language model (VLM) captioners replacing sparse alt-text by dense descriptions. A recaptioned corpus is a supervision distribution induced by a documented captioning policy ($π$), captioner ($V_c$), and source corpus ($C$). Length-correlated proxies miss caption-register artifacts and downstream T2I benchmarks entangle the corpus with training choices, so this distribution is hard to audit at corpus scale. We introduce a reusable matched-budget audit framework for recaptioned supervision distributions $D_{π,V_c,C}$: at a fixed text budget of $B = 64$ it reports a five-axis profile spanning prompt-side coverage, image-conditioned faithfulness, and caption-surface health, with claimed controllable basic units (CBU) as the common claim unit. We instantiate the framework on seven paired comparisons over five public source corpora. Across the four cross-corpus pairs, the released surface raises supported CBU per caption by $+3.39$ to $+6.36$ under both Qwen and Gemma Judges, and on CC12M the same framework exposes a long-vs-dense frontier that is consistent across both judges and four budgets. We release the audited multi-source recap corpus ($\approx$ 490M) together with the audit-artifact bundle.

</details>


### [54] [Two Clocks in Diffusion MLLMs: When Answers Stabilize Before Rationales Unfold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00953)
*Keuntae Kim,Yong Suk Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文区分了掩码扩散多模态大模型中答案候选的"回顾性稳定"与"令牌承诺"，并将这两个时间尺度与理由（rationale）生成进行对比分析。跨三个视觉问答基准的实验表明，答案稳定时大部分理由内容尚未写出，且块长度、EOS提示等因素会显著改变稳定与覆盖的比例关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在掩码扩散多模态大模型（MLLM）中，答案候选可能在理由尚未展开时就已趋于稳定，这带来一个关键问题：答案的"稳定"是否等同于模型的"承诺"？作者希望厘清答案稳定与理由生成这两条时间线之间的关系，并识别提示与结构参数对性能差异的驱动来源。

Method: 作者将日志中候选答案的回顾性稳定与真正的令牌承诺加以区分，考察这两个时间尺度相对于理由生成的位置。在三个视觉问答基准上进行实验，比较不同块长度（128 vs 8）、EOS抑制/启用提示等设置，并采用对称分解将性能变化归因于覆盖度与条件准确率，同时用匹配画布的图像消融测量视觉敏感性。

Result: 在单块、EOS抑制的LaViDa运行中，稳定时89.4%-98.1%的理由画布尚未写出；在V*Bench上，块长度从128降到8使该比例从89.4%变为1.7%。EOS启用的直接指令使Nemotron在M3CoT和ScienceQA上整体准确率分别提升15.0和19.5个百分点，但使LaViDa/V*Bench准确率下降11.0个点。对称分解显示每次变化中较大的绝对成分来自覆盖度而非条件准确率。

Conclusion: 这些测量将答案稳定、理由展开与视觉敏感性三者区分开来，并确认覆盖度是提示差异中更大的组成部分，说明答案稳定并不等同于理由完成或模型真正承诺。

Abstract: An answer candidate in a masked diffusion MLLM can stabilize while its rationale is still unfolding. We distinguish retrospective stabilization of the logged candidate from token commitment, and examine these two clocks relative to rationale generation. Analyzing our results across three visual question-answering benchmarks, we find that 89.4-98.1% of the rationale-side canvas remains unwritten at stabilization in single-block, EOS-suppressed LaViDa runs. On V*Bench, reducing block length from 128 to 8 changes this fraction from 89.4% to 1.7%, together with answer coverage and the eligible observation window. Under EOS-enabled prompting, direct instructions improve Nemotron's overall accuracy by 15.0 and 19.5 percentage points on M3CoT and ScienceQA, but reduce LaViDa/V*Bench accuracy by 11.0 points. A symmetric decomposition associates the larger absolute component of each change with coverage rather than conditional accuracy. Matched-canvas image ablations measure visual sensitivity alongside answer stabilization, separating the two temporal readouts. Together, these measurements distinguish answer stabilization, rationale unfolding, and visual sensitivity, and identify coverage as the larger component of the prompting differences.

</details>


### [55] [Video-Index: A Curated Meta-Benchmark for Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00960)
*Enxin Song,Yinuo Xu,Shusheng Yang,Wenhao Chai,Jiatao Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"攻击金字塔"（五个层级的捷径攻击），审计115个视频基准，发现大量基准可通过答案选项、问题文本或部分视觉证据被"捷径"攻克，并据此构建了经过验证的硬核基准Video-Index（840题）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频基准应当衡量其所声称的能力，但现有模型可利用答案选项、问题文本或部分视觉证据等捷径取得高分，导致评测失真。因此需要系统性审计基准的"可攻击性"，并构建抗捷径的可复现基准。

Method: 提出"攻击金字塔"——五级捷径攻击，访问权限逐级递增；用其对115个视频基准进行审计；对112个基准的505,518个问答对进行筛选，构建受审计的题目池；利用智能体将评估需求转化为规范，并由带红队门控的确定性选择器组合出可复现的基准。

Result: 在35个基准上，完全不看帧的攻击者可达到接近全视频的准确率；在51个带时间探针的基准上，打乱帧仍保留中位96%的全视频准确率；63个基准中近重复问题至少占一半题目。最终发布Video-Index：四个能力组每组210道最难验证题，共840题、来自76个来源。固定输入下，Claude Opus 5以超过37个百分点的优势领先所有开源模型，智能体工具再增加约20个百分点，但所有系统在效率与准确率上仍有提升空间。

Conclusion: 现有视频基准普遍存在严重捷径漏洞，评测结果被高估。Video-Index通过攻击审计与红队门控提供抗捷径、可复现的评测集，揭示当前系统仍有较大提升空间。

Abstract: A video benchmark should reward the capability it claims to measure, yet models can exploit answer options, question text, or partial visual evidence. We introduce the attack pyramid, five levels of shortcut attacks with increasing access to each item, and audit 115 video benchmarks with it. On 35 benchmarks, attackers that never see a frame approach full-video accuracy. On 51 benchmarks with temporal probes, shuffled frames keep a median 96% of full-video accuracy. Near-duplicate questions make up at least half the items in 63 benchmarks. We screen 505,518 question-answer pairs from 112 of them into an audited pool. Agents turn evaluation requests into specifications, and a deterministic selector with a red-team gate composes reproducible benchmarks. We release Video-Index, the 210 hardest verified items under these attacks in each of four capability groups, 840 items from 76 sources. With the same fixed input, Claude Opus 5 outscores every open-source model by over 37 percentage points, and agent tools add about 20 more, yet all systems leave room to improve efficiency and accuracy. Blog: https://www.enxinsong.com/blog/video-index/ GitHub: https://github.com/Espere-1119-Song/Video-Index Hugging Face: https://huggingface.co/datasets/Video-Index/Video-Index

</details>


### [56] [RelationVGGT: Visual Geometry Transformers for 3D Spatial Relation Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00970)
*Minsu Kim,Jaesung Choe,Jiwoo Lee,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Seon Joo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出"3D空间关系分割"这一新任务，在无需相机位姿的前馈多视角设定下，根据视觉指定的主体和关系文本查询分割目标，并提出RelationVGGT框架及自动化标注流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D重建已从前馈推理发展，语义场景理解也随之进步，但现有方法局限于以物体为中心的感知，忽略了物体间的空间关系。因此作者希望在无需逐场景优化和已知相机位姿的前馈多视角场景中，实现空间关系层面的分割。

Method: 提出RelationVGGT框架：融合视觉基础模型的语义特征与3D几何基础模型的几何感知表示，并利用关系transformer进行以主体为条件、跨视角的关系预测。同时基于ScanNet++构建了由VLM和LLM驱动的全自动标注流程，用于可扩展地生成该新任务的训练数据。

Result: 该研究定义了3D空间关系分割任务，并给出了相应的前馈解决方案与可扩展的数据生成方式，使模型无需逐场景优化或已知相机位姿即可跨视角分割关系目标。

Conclusion: 论文将语义场景理解从以物体为中心的感知拓展到物体间空间关系，提出了前馈、无位姿的多视角关系分割任务与方法，并提供自动化标注流程以支撑大规模训练。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D reconstruction have progressed from per-scene optimization to feed-forward inference, and semantic scene understanding has followed suit -- yet existing methods remain confined to object-centric perception, neglecting spatial relations between objects. We formulate 3D spatial relation segmentation in a feed-forward, pose-free multi-view setting: given a visually specified subject and a relational text query, the model segments the target across views without receiving its category name. To this end, we propose RelationVGGT, a novel feed-forward framework that integrates semantic features from a visual foundation model with geometry-aware representations from a 3D geometry foundation model and leverages a relation transformer for subject-conditioned, cross-view relation prediction -- requiring neither per-scene optimization nor known camera poses. We additionally provide a fully automated annotation pipeline built on ScanNet++ with VLMs and LLMs, enabling scalable training data generation for this new task.

</details>


### [57] [Concept Driven Domain Adaptation: Finding an Abstract Needle in a Haystack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00973)
*Haiming Zhao,Tai Wang,Kun Zhang,Xicheng Peng,Zhiyang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"概念到示例的视频检索"任务及CDDA三阶段框架，通过概念作为中间语义锚点适配双塔视觉-语言模型，在中学物理检索基准上优于多个多模态基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学教师检索纪录片片段时往往不是描述屏幕上的内容，而是查询他们想要讲授的抽象概念。现有基于语言的视频片段检索方法通常假设查询描述的是可观察事件，无法满足教学场景中"用具体视觉现象实例化抽象科学原理"的需求，由此产生了"概念到示例视频检索"这一抽象针-大海捞针式的问题。

Method: 提出概念驱动域适应（CDDA）三阶段框架，用于将双塔视觉-语言模型适配到概念级检索。它将概念作为中间语义锚点：(1) 用教科书与教师手册中的示例-概念对来结构化文本嵌入空间；(2) 在冻结视觉编码器下将该概念感知几何结构迁移到纪录片视觉上；(3) 用稀疏的视觉概念监督联合适配两个编码器。从几何角度看，这种分阶段对齐缩小了文本-概念与视觉-概念的角度差距。

Result: 在自建中学物理检索基准上，CDDA在面向教学的概念检索上优于包括Qwen3-VL-Embedding-2B在内的多个有竞争力的多模态基线，同时在适配后仍保持具体的图文匹配能力。

Conclusion: CDDA通过概念锚点的分阶段域适应有效弥合了抽象概念与具体视觉证据之间的鸿沟，在提升概念级检索的同时保留了预训练模型的图文对齐能力，为面向教学场景的视频检索提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Science teachers frequently search for documentary excerpts not by describing what appears on screen, but by querying the abstract concepts they intend to teach. This use case exposes a limitation of existing language-based video moment retrieval methods, which typically assume that queries describe observable events, whereas instructional search requires retrieving concrete visual phenomena that instantiate an underlying scientific principle. We study this setting as concept-to-example video retrieval, an abstract-needle-in-a-haystack problem where compact curriculum concepts must be grounded in temporally sparse documentary evidence. To bridge this abstraction gap, we propose Concept-Driven Domain Adaptation (CDDA), a three-stage framework for adapting two-tower vision-language models to concept-level retrieval. CDDA treats concepts as intermediate semantic anchors: it first structures the textual embedding space with textbook and teacher-handbook example-concept pairs, then transfers this concept-aware geometry to documentary visuals under a frozen visual encoder, and finally jointly adapts both encoders with sparse visual concept supervision. From a geometric perspective, this staged alignment reduces text-concept and vision-concept angular gaps, thereby encouraging concept-level adaptation while preserving the pretrained model's concrete image description alignment. On a curated middle-school physics retrieval benchmark, CDDA achieves stronger pedagogically oriented concept retrieval than several competitive multimodal baselines, including Qwen3-VL-Embedding-2B, while maintaining concrete image-text matching after adaptation.

</details>


### [58] [VIEScore2: Unified Image Evaluation with Spatially Grounded Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00994)
*Xianda Du,Max Ku,Weiming Ren,Zhi Rui Tam,Chunlin Ren,Ping Nie,Min-Hung Chen,Wenhu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VIEScore2是一个统一图像生成与编辑评测器，以N×N网格联合预测质量分数与缺陷位置，通过GRPO后训练提升缺陷定位，主基准整体分数SRCC达0.601，优于强零样本VLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成图像评测器通常只给出单一标量质量分数，无法指出支撑该分数的图像区域，缺乏可解释性与空间定位能力，且难以对不同来源的空间监督信号进行统一建模。

Method: 将图像表示为N×N网格，单次前向同时预测质量分数与缺陷位置；采用文本原生网格表示作为异构空间监督的统一接口，并支持可直接验证的后训练目标。训练集含38K样本，覆盖仅打分、仅定位及联合监督，任务涵盖生成与编辑。先做监督微调，再用GRPO优化缺陷定位，奖励由单元格级Dice重叠、分数准确率和输出格式有效性组合。另用无参数解析器将结构化预测转为可读解释。

Result: 主评测套件上整体分数SRCC为0.601，高于匹配输入下最强零样本通用VLM基线Gemini-3-Flash的0.491。缺陷定位方面，在六个基准中的三个上按逐图网格IoU超过通用VLM与专用空间评测器，在五个上进入前三，且包含训练来源之外的数据集。

Conclusion: 统一分数预测与缺陷定位的网格化评测框架可同时提供量化质量评分和可解释的空间诊断，并在打分与定位两类指标上均优于现有基线，具备跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Existing synthetic image evaluators typically provide only a scalar quality score and do not identify the image regions that support it. We introduce VIEScore2, a unified evaluator for image generation and editing tasks with optional conditioning images. VIEScore2 represents an image as an N x N grid and jointly predicts quality scores and defect locations in a single model pass. Its text-native grid representation provides a common interface for heterogeneous spatial supervision and enables directly verifiable post-training objectives. We train on 38K examples spanning score-only, localization-only, and joint supervision across generation and editing tasks. Starting from supervised fine-tuning, we further apply GRPO to improve defect localization using rewards that combine cell-level Dice overlap, score accuracy, and output-format validity. A parameter-free parser converts the structured predictions into readable explanations. On the primary suite, VIEScore2 achieves an overall-score SRCC of 0.601, compared with 0.491 for Gemini-3-Flash, the strongest zero-shot general-purpose VLM baseline under matched inputs. For defect localization, VIEScore2 outperforms both general-purpose VLMs and specialized spatial evaluators on three of six benchmarks in per-image grid IoU and ranks among the top three on five, including datasets beyond its training sources.

</details>


### [59] [Watch Your Speech: Text-aware Video-to-Speech Synthesis with Textual Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01012)
*Gunwoo Lee,Yoori Oh,Yoseob Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WYS框架，引入文本条件作为显式语言线索，缓解视频到语音合成中的一对一多映射问题，在LRS2/LRS3上实现SOTA的视听同步性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频到语音合成的根本挑战在于一对多映射：仅凭视觉动态往往不足以唯一确定对应的语音内容，存在视觉歧义。为缓解该问题，作者希望引入文本条件作为显式语言线索。

Method: WYS框架包含一个基于注意力的嵌入融合模块，将文本上下文与视频序列协同融合；并采用条件流匹配（conditional flow matching）目标实现高保真语音生成。

Result: 在LRS2和LRS3数据集上，WYS在视听同步指标（LSE-C/D）上取得新的SOTA，同时保持极具竞争力的文本准确率（WER）。主观评测表明生成语音接近人类自然度。

Conclusion: 文本条件在内容可控的视频到语音合成中有效，WYS验证了其缓解视觉歧义、提升同步性与自然度的能力。

Abstract: Video-to-speech synthesis aims to generate natural-sounding speech from silent talking-face videos while ensuring phonetic accuracy. A fundamental challenge in this task is the inherent one-to-many mapping problem, where visual dynamics often lack sufficient information to uniquely determine the corresponding utterance. To address this, we propose Watch Your Speech (WYS), a video-to-speech synthesis framework that incorporates textual conditioning as an explicit linguistic cue to mitigate visual ambiguity. Our framework features an attention-based embedding fusion module that synergistically integrates textual context with video sequences, coupled with a conditional flow matching objective for high-fidelity speech generation. Extensive experiments on the LRS2 and LRS3 datasets demonstrate that WYS achieves superior performance, establishing new state-of-the-art results in audio-visual synchronization (LSE-C/D) while maintaining highly competitive textual accuracy (WER). Subjective evaluations further confirm that our model generates speech with near-human naturalness, validating the effectiveness of textual conditioning in content-controlled video-to-speech synthesis. Project page: https://github.com/gunwoo5034/Watch-your-Speech

</details>


### [60] [VASC: Value-Aware Sparse Attention with Cross-Layer Memory for Efficient 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01013)
*Junyi Wu,Fanqing Kong,Leyang Chen,Shaoqiu Zhang,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VASC，一种免训练的稀疏注意力方法，融合价值感知块选择与跨层记忆，用于加速VGGT等前馈3D视觉模型，在保持甚至提升位姿估计与重建质量的同时实现最高2.29倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈3D视觉模型（如VGGT）虽统一了相机估计与稠密场景重建，但其二次全局注意力使长图像序列的计算开销高昂；而现有稀疏方法倾向于选择注意力权重高但价值冗余的区域，效率与效果均受限。

Method: 提出免训练的稀疏注意力方法VASC，包含两个核心机制：(1) 价值感知块选择，将池化后的query-key相关性与邻域价值对比度结合，降低冗余的同时保留与查询相关且具有区分度的内容；(2) 执行感知的跨层记忆，跨层追踪未被满足的需求，并根据实际执行情况更新状态，使此前未被充分服务的块在固定计算预算下也能参与竞争。

Result: 在7Scenes和NeuralRGB-D数据集上，基于VGGT与π³进行实验，与FasterVGGT相比，位姿估计和重建质量均有提升，相比稠密VGGT推理速度最高提升2.29倍。

Conclusion: VASC作为一种免训练稀疏注意力方法，通过价值感知选择与跨层记忆有效缓解了前馈3D视觉模型的注意力冗余与计算瓶颈，在精度和效率之间取得了更优平衡，代码已开源。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D vision models such as VGGT have achieved remarkable progress, unifying camera estimation and dense scene reconstruction in a single pass. However, their quadratic global attention makes long image sequences expensive, while existing sparse methods may favor highly attended yet value-redundant regions. To address these limitations, we introduce VASC, a training-free sparse attention method combining value-aware block selection and execution-aware cross-layer memory. Our value-aware block selection integrates pooled query--key relevance with neighboring value contrast, reducing redundancy while preserving query-relevant and distinctive content. Cross-layer memory tracks unserved demand across layers and updates this state according to actual execution, enabling previously underserved blocks to compete under a fixed computation budget. Experiments on 7Scenes and NeuralRGB-D with VGGT and $π^3$ demonstrate improved pose estimation and reconstruction quality compared with FasterVGGT, together with up to $2.29\times$ faster inference than dense VGGT. Code is available at https://github.com/kosakayamahoo-design/VASC.

</details>


### [61] [FutureWorlds: Learning Robotic World Models from Alternative Futures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01019)
*Hao Wu,Shengju Qian,Weiyan Wang,Fan Xu,Fan Zhang,Yuanpeng He,Qingsong Wen,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FutureWorlds 框架，统一候选构建、历史维护与相对质量学习，并设计 MemSPO 优化机器人世界模型，在多个数据集上显著提升长时预测质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将替代性预测转化为有效学习信号存在困难：相似候选难以提供有信息量的质量比较，而发散轨迹需要持续维护各自的历史。

Method: 基于多模态离散自回归模型，使用多样化束搜索在强化学习中构建兼顾置信度与多样性的候选未来；采用候选专属的有界记忆保存场景状态，确保生成与策略评分使用一致的历史；提出 MemSPO（记忆条件搜索引导策略优化），将视频轨迹奖励转化为组相对优势以优化世界模型。

Result: 在 RT-1、BridgeV2、RoboCasa 上，32 帧预测的 LPIPS 相对各自最强基线分别降低 14.78%、20.84%、9.12%；固定评估配置下仅 200 次 MemSPO 更新即可进一步提升生成质量，并支持超出训练时域的持续预测；记忆消融、解码敏感性分析与光流评估表明收益还体现在更准确的运动预测与更一致的对象状态。

Conclusion: FutureWorlds 通过统一候选构建、记忆维护与相对质量学习，有效提升机器人世界模型的长期预测质量与运动一致性。

Abstract: Robotic world models predict action-conditioned future scenes, providing a foundation for understanding action outcomes. However, turning alternative predictions into useful learning signals remains challenging: similar candidates limit informative quality comparisons, while diverging trajectories require persistent maintenance of their individual histories. We introduce FutureWorlds, a framework that unifies candidate construction, history maintenance, and learning from relative quality. Built on a multimodal discrete autoregressive model, FutureWorlds uses diverse beam search during reinforcement learning to construct candidate futures that balance confidence and diversity. Candidate-specific bounded memory preserves scene states and ensures that generation and policy scoring use matching histories. We further propose MemSPO (Memory-Conditioned Search-Guided Policy Optimization), which converts video trajectory rewards into group-relative advantages to optimize the world model. On RT-1, BridgeV2, and RoboCasa, FutureWorlds reduces LPIPS for 32-frame predictions by 14.78%, 20.84%, and 9.12%, respectively, relative to the strongest baseline on each dataset. Under fixed evaluation configurations, only 200 MemSPO updates further improve generation quality and support continued prediction beyond the training horizon. Memory ablations, decoding sensitivity analysis, and optical-flow evaluation show that these gains extend beyond visual quality to more accurate motion prediction and more consistent object states. Project page and code: https://github.com/Alexander-wu/FutureWorlds.

</details>


### [62] [Towards Automatic Video Annotation with ASH: Zero-Shot Open-Vocabulary Multi-Object Tracking and Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01022)
*Arash Rocky,Q. M. Jonathan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GPT与ASH两个模块，将SAM3扩展为可处理任意长度视频、支持多类别开放词汇的全自动跟踪标注流程SAM3-ASH，在零样本条件下达到MOTS20的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于记忆注意力的视频实例分割（VIS）方法虽具备强零样本跟踪能力，但内存需求大，只能处理短视频片段；同时其单提示推理设计使多类别开放词汇跟踪的计算代价过高，难以实现全自动化的任意视频跟踪标注。

Method: 提出两个贡献：(1) Generalized Presence Token (GPT)，通过虚拟提示批处理重构SAM3推理流程，使其可同时处理N个文本提示，将图像编码成本从O(N)降至O(1)，且不修改任何已学习组件；(2) Annotation and Segmentation Handler (ASH)，通过重叠时间分块与基于IoU的块间身份匹配，将任意记忆注意力VIS跟踪器扩展到任意长度序列，无需数据集特定训练。二者实例化于SAM3得到SAM3-ASH。

Result: 在完全零样本条件下，SAM3-ASH在MOTS20上取得SOTA HOTA，并在另外七个基准上与训练专用方法保持竞争力，同时峰值GPU内存消耗低于25 GB。

Conclusion: 该方法为可扩展、免训练的全自动视频标注建立了实用基线，兼顾性能与内存效率。

Abstract: Memory-attention-based Video Instance Segmentation (VIS) methods have demonstrated strong zero-shot tracking capability, yet their substantial memory requirements confine them to short video clips and their single-prompt inference design makes multi-category open-vocabulary tracking computationally prohibitive. This work introduces two contributions toward fully automated tracking annotation of arbitrary video. The Generalized Presence Token (GPT) reformulates SAM3's inference pipeline to process N text prompts simultaneously via virtual prompt batching, reducing image encoding cost from O(N) to O(1) with no modifications to any learned component. The Annotation and Segmentation Handler (ASH) extends any memory-attention VIS tracker to sequences of arbitrary length through overlapping temporal chunks with IoU-based inter-chunk identity matching, requiring no dataset-specific training. Instantiated on SAM3, the resulting pipeline -- SAM3-ASH -- achieves state-of-the-art HOTA on MOTS20 under fully zero-shot conditions and remains competitive with trained specialists across seven additional benchmarks, while peak GPU memory consumption stays below 25 GB, establishing a practical baseline for scalable, training-free automated video annotation.

</details>


### [63] [Bootstrapping Video Interaction Generation with Synthetic State Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01039)
*Jiho Jang,Jinyoung Kim,Nojun Kwak,Kyungjune Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可控交互合成数据生成框架，配合 State-Guided Sampling 采样技术与自动评估系统，微调后显著提升视频模型的合理交互生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型虽能合成高保真视频，但难以刻画合理的物理交互及状态转移，这成为机器人与 VR/AR 应用的关键瓶颈。

Method: 构建基于结构化分类法与先进图像编辑模型的可扩展合成数据集，生成明确的“起始”和“结束”状态图像作为视觉锚点；提出 State-Guided Sampling（SGS）采样技术，利用锚点生成无缝视频并缓解朴素条件生成的伪影；同时开发并验证与人类判断一致的自动评估系统。

Result: 实验表明，在自建数据集上对基础模型进行微调，可显著增强其生成合理交互的能力。

Conclusion: 该工作通过可控交互数据生成、状态引导采样与自动评估，为提升视频模型物理交互生成质量提供了可扩展方案。

Abstract: While recent video generative models can synthesize high-fidelity videos, they struggle to portray plausible physical interactions and the resulting state transitions, a critical bottleneck for applications in robotics and VR/AR. To address this, we introduce a framework to generate a scalable synthetic dataset of controllable interactions. Our pipeline leverages a structured taxonomy and state-of-the-art image editing models to create explicit `start' and `end' state images, which serve as visual anchors for the interaction. To generate a seamless video utilizing these anchors, we propose State-Guided Sampling (SGS), a novel sampling technique that mitigates artifacts common in naive conditional generation. Furthermore, we develop and validate a new automated evaluation system that aligns with human judgments to ensure data quality. Experiments show that fine-tuning a base model on our dataset significantly enhances its ability to generate plausible interactions.

</details>


### [64] [Towards Subject Consistency over Dynamic Subject Sets in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01052)
*Tongcheng Zhang,Jun Zhu,Jianfei Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出动态主体集合下的视频生成主体一致性评测框架 DynSC-Eval，并基于其构建奖励进行后训练优化，显著降低不一致性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着视频生成时长增加，主体一致性应在动态主体集合上评估；现有指标难以捕捉长时间视频中的一致性问题。

Method: DynSC-Eval 在主体可见生命周期内动态跟踪合格主体，用六个对象级指标衡量局部连续性与全局身份保持，并显式检测不一致事件；设计注入不一致事件的合成实验验证敏感性；基于该框架构建奖励，在自动驾驶测试台用 DiffusionNFT 后训练，并通过课程学习扩展到更长时长。

Result: 合成实验证明 DynSC-Eval 灵敏且揭示现有指标局限；在 5s、15s、60s 生成上发现被传统指标掩盖的显著差异；5s 生成时六项指标平均降低 13.82%（Wan-2.1-1.3B）和 5.66%（SANA-2B），I2V 模型 ReSim 也有提升；扩展到 10s 和 30s 后一致性优化仍有效。

Conclusion: 动态主体集合评估能更有效衡量长视频主体一致性，且可作为奖励用于后训练，在保持其他能力的同时提升一致性。

Abstract: We argue that as video generation extends to longer durations, subject consistency should be evaluated over \textit{dynamic subject sets}. We therefore introduce \textbf{DynSC-Eval}, an evaluation framework that dynamically tracks eligible subjects throughout their visible lifespans and measures local continuity and global identity preservation using six complementary object-level metrics, with explicit detection of inconsistency events. To validate its effectiveness, we design synthetic experiments that actively inject inconsistency events, demonstrating both the sensitivity of DynSC-Eval and the limitations of existing metrics. Evaluations of diverse models on 5s, 15s, and 60s video generation further reveal substantial subject consistency differences that are obscured by conventional metrics. Beyond evaluation, we construct rewards from DynSC-Eval and apply DiffusionNFT post-training in an autonomous-driving testbed. On 5s generation, our approach reduces the six inconsistency metrics by an average of 13.82\% for Wan-2.1-1.3B and 5.66\% for SANA-2B, with improvements also observed on the I2V model ReSim. Qualitative comparisons further demonstrate the effectiveness of our method. We then extend generation to 10s and 30s through curriculum learning and show that consistency optimization remains effective while largely preserving other capabilities.

</details>


### [65] [HierGF: Hierarchical Gaussian Fields via Geometry-perception Message Passing for Sparse-view 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01056)
*Bi'an Du,Zhimin Zhang,Daizong Liu,Baoquan Chen,Wei Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出分层高斯场（HierGF），从分层几何感知视角解决稀疏视图三维重建中的多视图一致性与欠采样区域缺失问题，通过自生成伪监督、可学习置信网络和几何一致稠密化模块提升重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视图三维重建在AR/VR、文化遗产数字化和机器人等应用中十分常见，但仅有少量随机视角可用。稀疏视图包含的三维信息有限，带来两大挑战：可用图像太少难以建立多视图一致性；视角覆盖不足导致欠采样区域信息缺失、物体结构缺失。现有方法多依赖有限的重投影误差和正则项，易过拟合单一视角并产生跨视图外观不一致，在几何欠采样区域依赖启发式密度控制，缺乏可靠引导，导致模糊和结构空洞。

Method: 提出分层高斯场（HierGF），从分层几何感知视角重新审视稀疏视图重建，将有限观测转化为超越固定先验和启发式密度控制的可靠自生成监督。具体而言，通过两阶段几何感知骨干网络，将粗三维几何信息和额外二维生成先验转换为结构化伪监督，从而在极少输入视图下增强多视图一致性。此外引入可学习置信网络，将梯度引导至跨视图一致内容，并设计几何一致稠密化模块以改善多视图对齐和欠采样区域的重建。

Result: 摘要未提供具体定量实验结果，主要阐述了方法在增强多视图一致性、改善多视图对齐和欠采样区域重建方面的设计效果。

Conclusion: HierGF通过分层几何感知将有限观测转化为自生成监督，结合可学习置信网络与几何一致稠密化模块，有效应对稀疏视图重建中的多视图不一致和欠采样缺失问题。

Abstract: Sparse view 3D reconstruction is an important and common scenario in multimedia applications, such as augmented reality/virtual reality (AR/VR) content creation, cultural heritage digitization, and certain robotic applications, where only a limited number of randomly captured views may be available. However, sparse views contain only limited 3D information, posing two major challenges:1) too few images are available for matching, making it difficult to build multi-view consistency; 2) insufficient view coverage leads to a lack of information in under-sampled regions, resulting in missing parts of object structure. Existing methods mostly still rely on limited reprojection errors and regularization terms, which are prone to overfitting to a single view and inconsistent appearances across views. In geometrically under-sampled regions, they often rely on heuristic density control, lacking reliable guidance and often resulting in blurring and structural holes.To address these issues, this paper proposes Hierarchical Gaussian Fields (HierGF), which revisits sparse-view reconstruction from a hierarchical geometry-perception perspective and converts limited observations into reliable self-generated supervision beyond fixed priors and heuristic density control. In particular, we transform coarse 3D geometric information and additional 2D generative priors into structured pseudo-supervision through a two-stage geometry-perception backbone network, thereby enhancing multi-view consistency with very few input views. In addition, we introduce a learnable confidence network to guide gradients toward cross-view consistent content, and a geometrically consistent densification module to improve the reconstruction of multi-view alignment and under-sampled regions.

</details>


### [66] [Overcoming Kernel Redundancy for Scaling Logic Gate Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01069)
*Sejin Park,Hongjae Lee,Changwoo Han,Seung-Won Jung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究可微逻辑门网络的宽度缩放行为，发现朴素宽度缩放会引入逻辑核冗余导致性能饱和，提出动态逻辑核框架以提升核利用率与多样性，从而提高精度与参数效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微逻辑门网络作为高效替代方案受到关注，但其缩放行为研究不足。而深度神经网络中扩大模型容量通常带来性能提升。这引出关键问题：逻辑门网络能否获得类似的缩放收益？本文以宽度为主要缩放轴进行系统分析。

Method: 观察发现朴素宽度缩放会导致逻辑核之间出现冗余，限制额外核的有效使用并造成性能饱和。为此提出动态逻辑核框架，通过促进核组间的专门化来重组核利用，借助输入相关的核路由更好地利用增加的宽度，并保证路由与计算在推理时完全以门级布尔运算实现。进一步发现核冗余在第一个门级最为明显，提出早期阶段动态逻辑核策略，将该层作为适配重点。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法提高了核利用率并增加了核多样性，在提升精度的同时实现了更好的参数效率。

Conclusion: 宽度缩放并非在逻辑门网络中自动带来收益，核冗余是主要瓶颈。通过动态逻辑核与早期阶段适配策略，可在门级布尔运算约束下有效利用增加的宽度，实现精度与参数效率的双重提升。

Abstract: Differentiable logic gate networks, which operate using only logic gates, have recently attracted attention as an efficient alternative to conventional neural networks. However, despite their efficiency, the scaling behavior of logic gate networks remains underexplored. By contrast, scaling model capacity is a central design principle in deep neural networks and typically leads to improved performance. This discrepancy raises a key question: Can similar scaling benefits also be achieved in logic gate networks? In this work, we focus on width as a primary scaling axis and conduct a systematic analysis of its behavior in logic gate networks. We observe that naive width scaling often introduces redundancy among logic kernels, limiting the effective use of additional kernels and leading to performance saturation. To address this limitation, we propose a dynamic logic kernel framework that reorganizes kernel utilization by promoting specialization across kernel groups. This enables the network to better utilize increased width via input-dependent kernel routing, while ensuring that both routing and computation are implemented entirely with gate-level Boolean operations at inference time. We further find that kernel redundancy is most pronounced at the first gate level, motivating an early-stage dynamic logic kernel strategy that concentrates adaptation at this level. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach improves kernel utilization and increases kernel diversity, leading to higher accuracy with improved parameter efficiency.

</details>


### [67] [Ego2Act: Evaluating Goal-Directed Manipulation in Egocentric Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01092)
*Patrick Amadeus Irawan,Iskandar Muda Rizky Parlambang,Rava Maulana,Qinrong Cui,Erland Hilman Fuadi,Zayd M. K. Zuhri,Nanda Ryaas Absar,Ahmed Elshabrawy,Wilfried Ariel Mulyawan,Shoubin Yu,Yue Zhang,Mohit Bansal,Alham Fikri Aji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Ego2Act基准，包含110个真实日常任务、2,640个视频，用于评估视频生成模型在高层次目标下的第一人称多步操作模拟能力，并配套无参考评测流程Ego2ActJudge。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型被用作具身规划与学习的世界模拟器时，不仅要生成视觉上吸引人的画面，还需预测执行目标导向动作时环境如何动态演变。现有基准多关注单一短动作或逐步指令，缺乏对多步物理推理的评估，尤其是需要规划以模拟正确执行、通过多种真实操作达成高层目标的第一人称视频生成。

Method: 提出Ego2Act，目标导向基准，涵盖110个真实世界任务、2,640个视频，涉及日常场景，变化物体杂乱度与多步复杂度。给定初始场景图像和高层目标，评估模型能否生成真实的第一人称视频，展示手操作物体完成任务。同时提出Ego2ActJudge，一种无参考评测流程，在任务完成度和物理合理性评估上与人类共识对齐优于相关基线。

Result: 模型生成的模拟常跳过或部分执行步骤，导致后续步骤缺少依赖状态，最终无法完成任务目标；模型在细粒度物理动态上持续失败，尤其在复杂物体操作和持久世界建模方面。

Conclusion: Ego2Act为推进视频模型迈向物理合理、目标导向的模拟提供了严格测试平台。

Abstract: Video generation models are increasingly being explored as world simulators for embodied planning and learning. To do so effectively, these models must not only generate visually appealing frames, but also predict how environments dynamically evolve when executing goal-directed actions. While evaluating these capabilities is crucial, existing benchmarks focus mainly on single short actions or step-by-step instructions. This leaves multi-step physical reasoning underexplored, especially in egocentric video generation that requires planning to simulate proper execution to accomplish high-level goals by carrying out multiple real-world manipulations. We introduce Ego2Act, a goal-directed benchmark featuring 2,640 videos from 110 real-world tasks across day-to-day settings, varying object clutter and multi-step complexity. Given an initial scene image and a high-level goal, Ego2Act evaluates whether video generation models can produce realistic egocentric videos of a hand manipulating objects to carry out the task. To support scalable evaluation, we also introduce Ego2ActJudge, a reference-free evaluation pipeline that achieves better task completion and physics plausibility evaluation alignment with human consensus compared to relevant baselines. Our findings reveal that models' generated simulations often skip or partially execute steps, leaving later steps missing dependent states, which leads to unfulfilled goal. Furthermore, models consistently fail at fine-grained physical dynamics, particularly during complex object manipulation and persistent world modeling. We hope Ego2Act provides a rigorous testbed for advancing video models toward physically plausible, goal-directed simulation.

</details>


### [68] [MVDG: Efficient Multi-view 3D Disambiguation on Unconstrained Real-World Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01098)
*Hanyuan Xiao,Gonglin Chen,Haolin Xiong,Wenbin Teng,Haiwei Chen,Yajie Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MVDG，基于3D基础模型VGGT的多视图消歧框架，联合推理任意数量多视图图像，以单次编解码替代成对比较，降低O(n^2)复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D重建与视觉定位中，视觉相似却不同的3D表面（doppelgangers）造成的"幻觉匹配"是规模化野外重建的根本障碍。已有成对分类器方案限制多视图上下文推理，且下游SfM推理复杂度为O(n^2)。

Method: 基于VGGT构建多视图消歧框架，引入3D感知多视图特征，在单次前向中编码解码多视图。发现直接多视图微调VGGT在噪声监督下不稳定，受Doppelgangers标签歧义启发，从AerialMegaDepth构造伪成对训练集，用采样子集微调获得稳定优化与泛化。另构建伪成对数据集用于高效验证，并推导常规SfM指标与该伪成对测试分类精度的预测关系。

Result: 方法达到与基线相当的成对准确率，同时在SfM准确率和推理速度上均有提升。

Conclusion: MVDG以多视图联合推理替代成对分类，兼顾精度、效率与泛化，为大规模3D重建中的doppelganger消歧提供了可扩展方案。

Abstract: Illusory matches between distinct yet visually similar 3D surfaces--doppelgangers--remain a fundamental obstacle for large-scale, in-the-wild 3D reconstruction and visual localization. Prior work mitigates this issue with pairwise classifiers, but this design limits multi-view contextual reasoning and incurs O(n^2) inference complexity for downstream structure-from-motion (SfM). We present MVDG, a scalable multi-view disambiguation framework built on the 3D foundation model VGGT, which jointly reasons over an arbitrary number of multiview images. By incorporating 3D-aware multi-view features, our method reduces dependence on pairwise comparisons by encoding and decoding views in a single pass. We further observe that direct multi-view fine-tuning of VGGT can be unstable under noisy supervision; motivated by label ambiguity in Doppelgangers, we construct a pseudo-pairwise training set from AerialMegaDepth and show that fine-tuning on sampled subsets yields stable optimization and strong generalization to held-out scenes. Finally, because full SfM evaluation (even with faster pipelines such as GLOMAP) remains expensive, we process a pseudo-pairwise dataset for efficient validation; we derive a predictive relationship between regular SfM metrics and the classification accuracy on this pseudo-pairwise test. Experiments show that our method achieves comparable pairwise accuracy while improving both SfM accuracy and inference speed over baselines.

</details>


### [69] [Affine-Aligned Atlas for Canonical Gaussian Construction in Video Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01114)
*Masaya Takabe,Hiroshi Watanabe,Sujun Hong,Tomohiro Ikai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"仿射图集规范高斯表示"(affine-atlas canonical Gaussian representation)，通过构建仿射对齐图集空间中的规范高斯并结合逐帧仿射变换先吸收全局运动，缓解大相机运动下规范表示与各帧错位的问题，可无缝集成到现有基于规范高斯的方法中且参数开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯的视频表示通常将视频分解为规范高斯(canonical Gaussians)与时间形变(temporal deformation)。但当视频存在大范围全局运动(如相机运动)时，规范表示会与单帧发生错位，从而加重时间形变模型的负担，导致重建质量下降。

Method: 在更大的仿射对齐图集空间(affine-aligned atlas space)中构造规范高斯；利用逐帧仿射变换在规范高斯构建之前吸收全局运动，从而缩小规范表示与目标帧之间的差距。该方法仅修改规范构建阶段，可集成进现有基于规范高斯的框架，额外参数开销可忽略。

Result: 实验表明，该方法提升了重建质量，尤其在含大范围相机运动的视频序列上效果更为明显。

Conclusion: 通过在规范高斯构建前引入仿射变换吸收全局运动，所提仿射图集表示有效缓解了错位问题，提升了含大相机运动视频的重建质量；由于只改动画规范构建环节，方法具备良好的通用性与低参数成本，易于推广至现有方法。

Abstract: Gaussian splatting has recently emerged as an efficient representation for images and videos due to its explicit structure and fast rendering capability. Existing Gaussian-based video representations often decompose a video into canonical Gaussians and temporal deformation. However, when a video contains large global motion such as camera movement, the canonical representation may become misaligned with individual frames, increasing the burden on the temporal deformation model. In this paper, we propose an affine-atlas canonical Gaussian representation, which constructs canonical Gaussians in a larger affine-aligned atlas space. Frame-wise affine transforms absorb global motion before canonical Gaussian construction, reducing the gap between the canonical representation and target frames. Since the proposed method only modifies the canonical construction stage, it can be integrated into existing canonical-Gaussian-based methods with negligible additional parameter cost. Experiments show that our method improves reconstruction quality especially for sequences with large camera motion.

</details>


### [70] [Open Vocabulary Word Recognition From Transcribed Bangla Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01134)
*Faias Satter,Sk. Md. Masudul Ahsan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究针对手写孟加拉语单词识别难题，采用SSD+MobileNetV2、Faster R-CNN+InceptionResNetV2及二者集成模型进行训练测试，并引入改进的非极大值抑制，在自建9841张手写孟加拉语词图像数据集上，集成模型F1-score达92.61%，词级识别率达96.12%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCR可自动扫描并提取文本，减轻人工负担，但孟加拉语的可靠OCR方案稀缺，手写文本更是如此。单词识别是OCR流程中仅次于分词的关键第二阶段，若该阶段失败，整体OCR性能将很差。因此本研究旨在用深度学习识别手写孟加拉语单词图像。

Method: 采用三种目标检测模型：SSD+MobileNetV2、Faster R-CNN+InceptionResNetV2，以及两者的集成模型；引入改进的非极大值抑制（NMS）以提升结果有效性；构建包含9841张、来自不同书写者、风格多样的手写孟加拉语单词图像的自定义数据集进行训练与测试。

Result: 在测试数据集上评估三种模型，集成模型表现最佳，F1-score为92.61%；在词级层面，集成模型能在一定程度上正确识别96.12%的单词。
